Related boxes have no fill, thin gray borders, gray names, and related (「関連」) on line 2. Do not use dotted lines, already used for unchanged items and inferences. How hunks absent from reading order (such as deviations) use the related appearance with purple borders. History: On 2026-09-25, kept only reading-order boxes (to remove boxes where clicks did nothing), but restored them because effects of How changes became invisible
- Build the entire layout once with elkjs (`layered`; bands via `partitioning`). Fixed issues encountered in examples on 2026-09-24: lay out disconnected islands together (`separateConnectedComponents: false`; separate layouts ignore bands on islands and extend edges to the diagram's bottom); reverse upward edges (lower band → upper band) for layout, then restore for drawing; include edge points when determining diagram width/height. elkjs ignores partitions for edgeless nodes, so place those in bands manually
- Run elkjs in a browser Web Worker at render time (not in `core`; layout is geometry without meaning, so it belongs to rendering. Principle 5)
- Zoom: 50 / 70 / 100% (default 100%). Do not change zoom during motion (18.3). **Trackpad pinch zooms only the map** (2026-10-20). Range: 25%–200%. The pinched point stays under the fingers. Prevent whole-browser zoom over the map. Chrome and others send Ctrl-modified `wheel`; Safari sends `gesturestart` / `gesturechange` (`map/zoom.ts`). Two fingers without Ctrl still pan. Buttons light only at their exact zoom level
- **Always draw calls that skip layers as red dashed lines. Do not judge them** (legend: calls that skip layers (facts only) (「層を飛び越える呼び出し（事実のみ）」))
- Schema dependencies (save → migration) are dotted (not implemented; no mechanical edge derivation)
- Press a stop box to go to that stop (`course.jump`). Bundle boxes open the bundle in by purpose (`group.reveal`; filter by the course's How)
- **Pressing a related box keeps the current stop and replaces only the stop screen's code with that box's file (peek; `map.peek`)** (decided 2026-09-26; moved into courses on 2026-09-30). Close the drawer. Above code, show related: name, layer, changed/unchanged, course n (「関連 名前 層 変更/未変更 コース n」) and stop you were reading: X [return] (「読んでいた停留所: X ［戻る］」). Return via return (「戻る」) (`map.unpeek`) / Esc / moving to a stop. While peeking, forward / back / `j` / `k` / `o` only returns to the previous stop (no advance or no-issues mark). `v` and open in IDEA apply to the peeked hunk. **Can peek at files too** (2026-10-20): other-course files in the left tree (`course.peekFile`) and answer-line locations invisible at the current stop (3.10). Above code: peeking: filename, course n (「覗いている ファイル名 コース n」) (diffs belonging to no course: outside courses (「コースの外」); unchanged files: no diff) and stop you were reading: X [return `Esc`]; below: that file's diffs (no dimmed diffs; fold outside changed-line context; also unfold and highlight answer-line locations). Same return behavior as boxes. `v` and open in IDEA apply to the answer-line hunk, or the file's first hunk. Peeked item: `AppState.peek` (`{ kind: 'symbol', symbolId }` or `{ kind: 'file', path, line? }`)

Ask (`Q`) is in 3.10 (focuses the stop screen's right-column input; summary uses ask in the right column to open a drawer). Overview is the entry map tab (3.1); wrap-up is 3.9.

### 3.4 Diff list

Goal: Entry, by purpose, and courses cannot guarantee coverage, so verify review completeness in a familiar UI. Enter through top-bar diff list (key `D`), wrap-up's open, or palette files.

Two columns: **Files** | **Diffs** (the comment-draft column moved to wrap-up (3.9) on 2026-10-03). The diff column lists every file in one stream and scrolls across files (2026-10-20; “one scrolling stream across files” below)

Added course-color circles, no-issues counts, and directory headings on 2026-10-03 (directory headings became a file tree on 2026-10-19) (decided by comparing examples; contents of the demo's “index.” **Do not use “index” in the UI**: it is a metaphor, and Japanese uses it only for books).

File column:
- Header: `N ファイル`, `問題なし n / N` (hunk counts), expand all (「すべて開く」), collapse all (「すべて閉じる」)
- Default width 340px (`clamp` below 1180px). Drag boundary to resize, double-click for default (2026-10-20; same as 3.3. `panes.diffTree`; minimum 220px, maximum 50vw)
- **IDEA-like file tree** (2026-10-19. Shape decided with working examples. Previously flat rows grouped by directory headings, with paths below `packagePrefix` and “under com.example”). Folders first; both sorted by name. **A folder containing only one folder joins with the next into one row** (IDEA's Compact Middle Packages; `kotlin/com/example`). Paths start at repository root (compacted rows shorten them, so do not shorten by `packagePrefix`). Vertical indent guides every 14px, disclosure arrows, folder and file icons (by extension: Kotlin, SQL, other). Folder rows have name and contained-file count. Press to toggle (`tree.toggle`; all: `tree.openAll` / `tree.closeAll`). Closed folders have aggregate file indicators at right. Open/closed state: `AppState.treeShut.diff` (not saved; all open on another analysis). Bright row = topmost file in diff column (2026-10-20. `AppState.diffFile`; follows scrolling). On selected-file change (`[` `]`, `j` `k`, ⌘K, and selections on other screens), open ancestor folders (do not reopen merely on screen round trips or scrolling) and scroll the bright row into view. `[` `]` and `j` `k` also use tree order (`groupFiles` sorted by `compareTreeOrder` in `fileTree.ts`; same in diff column). `[` `]` count from the bright row's file. Tree: `fileTree.ts`; rows: `components/FileTree.tsx` (shared with course's left side). Column width 340px (previously 280px; extra for indentation)
- Rows have course-color circles (Hows of file hunks; colors/numbers in 3.3), a red outside circle for any hunks belonging to no course, and an indicator at far right. `groupFiles` (`viewedCount`) in `files.ts`, and `components/FileList.tsx`
- **Indicators** (2026-10-13. Decided by comparing examples. `components/ViewMark.tsx`): No issues is a **circle** (all green / some half / none gray); GitHub Viewed (12.1) is a **check**; both is **circle with check** (half-circle with check if partial). If unviewed here but Viewed on GitHub, omit gray circle and show check only. Circle is 14px to hold the check (previously 8px dot). Hover: no issues 1 / 2 · Viewed on GitHub (「問題なし 1 / 2 · GitHub で Viewed」). GitHub Viewed is an indicator only; it does not mark no issues in ondina (do not extend a file-level mark to per-hunk no issues). No check for local analyses
- Follow-on counts `追従 n · 逸脱 n · 近い逸脱 n` moved to group rows in by purpose (2026-09-19; displayed here in M0)
- Show only the representative and deviations from follow-on groups; fold the rest (note in header)

Diff column:
- Header (column-wide actions): split/unified toggle (default split; `UiSettings.diffMode.d`; 3.8), show all `F` (opens all strips below; press again for near changes only (「変更の近くだけ」)), ask, `IDEA で開く`. Path and memos outside diff n (「差分の外のメモ n」) moved to per-file headings on 2026-10-20
- **One scrolling stream across files** (2026-10-20. Same as GitHub. Previously showed only the selected file's diff. Built and compared two designs, then used one as the base with two details from the other): One virtual list of all file diffs in tree order, with per-file heading rows. Headings contain paths and, if personal memos exist on this file's lines outside every diff list hunk (far from changes; writable on course stop screens), orange memos outside diff n (press to `comment.open` the first = course stop where its line is visible; an indicator to avoid silently hiding them. 2026-10-06). The current file's heading sticks to the top; the next file's heading pushes it off and replaces it (same as GitHub). Fixed 34px heading height; truncate long paths with …
  - Bright tree row is the topmost file, following scrolling upward or downward without lag. Pressing a tree file, `[` `]`, or a palette file puts its heading at the top (even pressing the same file again returns there)
  - Keep the selected diff (purple heading; target of `v`, `IDEA で開く`, ask) while visible (do not steal a clicked selection on a small scroll). Once invisible, select the topmost visible diff (`v` never applies to an invisible diff). Thus the bright tree row and purple heading may indicate different files
  - `j` `k` advance across files. Do not scroll if visible; otherwise scroll, avoiding leaving the previous file's end at the top (if destination lies within one screen of file start, put its file heading at top. When centering guide-answer lines or open-memo lines, likewise show nothing above file start)
  - At the bottom, if the final short file cannot reach the top, brighten that file in the tree while its selected diff is visible
  - Writing boxes stay open on scrolling or jumping to other files. Show all `F` applies to the whole column and persists across files. Switching `F` or split / unified returns to the selected diff (column-height changes would otherwise lose the reading place)
  - Mechanism: Move the list only when n advances in the reducer's one-shot `AppState.diffJump` signal (selected diff, split / unified, show all, or analysis changes; entering diff list; selecting a file). Do not move merely because selection followed scrolling. Each render, the list reports the topmost file and visible diffs (at least one row below the sticky heading) via command `diff.viewport` (scrolling is an action too, hence a command; principle 6. Only when content changes). The reducer follows selection only for reports after displaying the signaled destination (n matches the current signal), preventing programmatic scrolling from fighting following selection. Unchanged reports return identical state without rerendering. Scrolling does not count as “moved” (preserve writing box, highlighted lines, show all, tree folders; reducer `placeMoved`). Position calculations are pure functions without DOM reads (`viewportOf` / `revealPlan` / `stickyOf` in `diff/follow.ts`; heading height `FILE_HEAD_H` = virtual list `scrollPaddingStart`). Rows: `heads` in `buildRows` in `diff/rows.ts` (row-order `base` starts from 0 per file). Overlay sticky headings outside virtual rows; push-off changes style without React rerendering per pixel (`jump` in `diff/DiffList.tsx`). Do not collapse files, mark no issues per file, or open closed folders on scroll. By purpose preview and course screens remain single-file
- Per-hunk header: `@@ symbol` + course-color circles (red outside if no course), kind chip (`核` / `追従` / `差異あり` / `テスト`; deviations use `差異あり`, omitting chapter-8 distance (2026-10-13); close deviations red; no chip if kind absent), no issues button (viewed mark; same toggle as `v`, no issues ✓ (「問題なし ✓」) if set). `diff/DiffList.tsx`. Same component for by purpose preview and deviations in course summary
- Split view: old/new line numbers, additions green, removals red, hatch lines without counterparts. **Preserve syntax highlighting even on diff lines; change only background** (same as IDEA). Wrap long lines within their half-column (2026-10-11. Previously truncated with …, hiding code. Same in by purpose, courses, and diff list. Wrap at edges even mid-word; wrapping at spaces moves long words after indentation to the next line, leaving the first line blank)
- **Changed words within lines** (2026-10-30. Working examples compared word-level / character-level / no fill; chose word-level): In removed/added line pairs, give changed words a background stronger than the line color (like GitHub). Pair as in split view: each consecutive removed line with each immediately following consecutive added line, from the start. Same pairs in unified mode. Unpaired lines have only line color. Do not highlight pairs sharing fewer than 40% of non-whitespace characters, since they are mostly rewritten (such as `val promo: Promo? = null,` → `var discount: Money = Money.ZERO,`). Split tokens into runs of names/numbers, runs of whitespace, and single other characters; align for the longest shared token sequence (prefer matching identical tokens later; highlight added `${Instant.now()}` in `"[$level] $msg"` as a whole). Join changed tokens separated only by whitespace; omit whitespace at edges. For indentation-only changes, highlight whitespace. Search-hit colors take priority. Preserve syntax colors. Same in diff list, by purpose preview, course screen, and summary deviations. Compute in the UI from `hunk.lines` without changing analysis results, so old analyses gain it too. `diff/wordDiff.ts`
- **Fold lines far from changed lines** (2026-10-13. Unchanged lines were hard to read through. Show only near changes like GitHub, with the rest expandable. Working examples compared “20 lines at a time (same as GitHub) / all at once”; chose 20): Non-Kotlin files are one whole-file hunk; Kotlin hunks are declarations (sometimes entire classes), so hundreds of unchanged lines can occur. Keep 3 lines around changed lines; group distant unchanged lines in pale-blue `⋯ n 行` strips. Do not fold a single line (same height as a strip). Strip-left `↓` opens 20 lines from the top, `↑` from the bottom; a middle strip with at most 20 remaining lines opens all via one `↕`. At file start (whole file in by purpose preview), only `↑`; at file end, only `↓`. Strips never span hunk headings (always shown). Do not fold memo, AI-draft, writing-box, or guide-answer lines (3.10); keep their 3-line context too. Store opened lines in `AppState.fold` (file → row key; hunk lines use hunk id and lines index, outside-hunk lines use head line number), shared with by purpose preview and course stops (not saved; refold on another analysis). Show all uses `AppState.fullFile` (same state and `F` as course stop full file; command `file.full`); resets on screen changes (by purpose also resets on file reselection; diff list applies to whole column and does not reset. 2026-10-20). Strip buttons: `fold.open`. `diff/fold.ts` (calculation), `diff/FoldBand.tsx` (strip), `foldVirtual` in `diff/rows.ts`. Same strips in by purpose preview, course stops, and summary deviations
- **Search within diffs** (2026-10-27. Browser find could not locate offscreen text. Compared three fixes (custom search / remove virtualization and put all rows in DOM / text-only offscreen rows); chose custom search): The diff column is virtualized (18.5); offscreen and folded rows are absent from DOM and browser find cannot locate them. In diff list and by purpose preview (3.2), `⌘F` / `Ctrl+F` (`find.open`; configurable) opens a search field at column upper right (below sticky heading in diff list), without browser find. Search pre-fold row order (`findHits` in `diff/find.ts`): code lines (split view counts removed left and added right separately; outside-hunk lines count once), hunk-heading symbols, file-heading paths. Do not search memo or AI-draft boxes. Case insensitive. Count: n / m; no results: red not found (「見つかりません」). `Enter` next, `Shift+Enter` previous (wrap at end); `Esc` or × closes (retain text; select it when reopening; `⌘F` while open also focuses and selects text). Query change starts at first result. Unfold the current result (`foldVirtual` keep, like guide-answer lines; do not change `AppState.fold`) and scroll to center. Hits yellow, current hit orange `mark` (retain role colors). State: `AppState.find` (`open` / `query` / `at` = number incremented/decremented only by next/previous, wrapped by DiffList / FileView using count / `n` = refocus signal; not saved). Closes on screen change. While the definition drawer (`DefDrawer`) or unchanged-counterpart drawer (`RelDrawer`) is open, `⌘F` searches the drawer’s file on every screen, including courses (`findIn` determines the search target). The field says 「このファイルの中を検索」 (“Search within this file”), and matching rows stay unfolded. Opening or closing a drawer closes the search field. `Esc` closes search before the drawer. With no drawer open and no diff column (entry, courses, wrap-up, etc.), `findIn` returns null (`findable` is false), so `⌘F` remains browser find. Course-stop lines folded in strips remain unfindable through browser search
- Click symbols to jump within the tool (3.6)
- **Memos** (3.3. 2026-10-06): As on course stops, select lines via “+” on line-number hover (hold and drag up/down for ranges); writing box immediately below the last selected line, saved memos below that line too (edit/delete). Unified mode: “+” at before-change (base) line numbers, full-width memos. **Split mode: “+” at left line numbers for removed lines, right for added/unchanged lines** (send unchanged as head-side lines; same as course), **memos under that side's column** (base left, head right; same-row left/right memos side by side; base-to-head ranges use last line's side. On 2026-10-06, examples led to “same as GitHub split view”). Range endpoints use “file diff row order” (sum of lines positions in hunk order), identical in split and unified, preserving selections and drafts across modes. Selected-line colors in split view apply only to endpoint-side columns (head-to-head ranges do not color intervening removed lines). Scrolling or jumping to other diff list files preserves selection and writing box (2026-10-20; previously cleared on another file). Screen changes clear them. Row order is per file, so color only selected-file rows. Insert memo rows as virtual-list rows (`withMemos` in `diff/rows.ts`). Selecting lines or saving does not scroll back to the hunk heading. No “+” in by purpose preview or summary deviation diffs (not writing locations)
- **AI drafts** (2026-10-13. 3.3): Boxes at memo positions, accept/edit/discard in place (`withMemos` in `diff/rows.ts` also inserts drafts). For drafts on lines absent from diff list, file heading shows drafts outside diff n (「差分の外の下書き n」) (press to go where the line is visible; same as memos outside diff n). Not shown in by purpose preview or summary deviation diffs

### 3.5 Themes

- Official Catppuccin palettes (Latte / Frappé / Macchiato / Mocha). Switch UI and code with the same variables. Latte is light; the other three are dark
- Three choices (2026-10-22; previously one of four flavors)
  - **Theme (「テーマ」)** (`mode`): follow system (`system`; default) / light (`light`) / dark (`dark`)
  - **Light theme (「ライトのテーマ」)** (`light`): Light-mode flavor. Only Latte now, but use a dropdown to allow more (chosen by comparing examples)
  - **Dark theme (「ダークのテーマ」)** (`dark`): Dark-mode flavor (Frappé / Macchiato / Mocha)
- Follow system subscribes to OS appearance (`prefers-color-scheme`). OS light/dark changes (including macOS scheduled automatic switching) update the UI without reload. Before React renders, `index.html` briefly applies Latte or Mocha according to OS appearance (avoid a dark flash on a light OS)
- Keep flavor brightness in one table in `packages/web/src/theme.ts`. Dropdown contents, Mermaid brightness, and old-format conversion use it
- Selectable accent color (mauve default, blue, lavender, teal, peach, pink)
- Switch in settings modal (top-bar gear; 3.0). Until 2026-09-30, at rail bottom
- Save in `theme` in `.ondina/ui.json` (`{ mode, light, dark, accent }`; across PRs; 3.8). UI theme priority: `ui.json` > `theme` in `ondina.config.yaml` (chapter 11) > defaults (follow system / latte / mocha / mauve). Works without analysis (2026-10-11. Previously in per-PR `ReviewState`; without analysis, presses did nothing, and a changed headSha after push reset it)
- Pre-2026-10-22 `{ flavor, accent }` form (`ui.json` and yaml) is read as fixed light/dark for that flavor. Light flavors select light and populate `light`; dark flavors select dark and populate `dark`. Next save converts `ui.json` to the new form
- UI font Inter (Noto Sans JP for Japanese), code font JetBrains Mono
- IDEA New UI panel arrangement: background crust, panels base, subpanels mantle, borders surface0

### 3.6 Code jumps

- **Within the tool** (implemented 2026-09-25; moved to courses on 2026-09-30): On a course screen, `o` (or stop-screen callees button; `jump.callee`) lists targets called by the current stop's symbol (`to` of `Edge.kind: 'call'`; one per target) in the command palette. Each row: same short name as map, layer and destination in faint text. Selecting searches and jumps in this order (`calleeDest` in `palette.ts`):
  1. If a stop in the current course, go there
  2. If a stop in another course (first How in `tours` order), go to that course and stop
  3. If absent from all reading orders but has a diff, select that hunk in by purpose (clear How filter, expand collapsed bundle)
  4. If unchanged, keep the current stop and show the target in the unchanged-counterpart drawer (2026-10-11; faint text: no diff (show on right) (「差分なし（右に出す）」))
  5. Otherwise (target has no symbol information), just close palette. Keep the candidate with no destination (「飛び先なし」) (removing it would erase the distinction between “not called” and “cannot jump”)
  - Empty-list reasons differ: `edgesMeasured: false`: ? could not retrieve call relationships (…) (「? 呼び出し関係を取得できていません（…）」); absent field (such as file-level symbols): ? call relationships not retrieved (「? 呼び出し関係を取得していません」); retrieved zero: no callees (「呼んでいる相手なし」) (principle 2)
- **From names to definitions** (implemented 2026-10-29. Comparing examples led to “show on right,” with enlargement like callees. Extended to courses and by purpose the same day): On diff list, by purpose (preview), and course (stop screen, peeked-file and peeked-box code) screens, hold ⌘ and hover a navigable name for an underline; ⌘+click (Ctrl+click on Windows/Linux) goes to its definition (`def.go`). Destinations are `Analysis.defs` (9.2), queried from the language server's `textDocument/definition` during analysis. Three destination cases:
  1. If the line is already in the code column, scroll to it, highlight (`focusLine`; unfold folded lines too), and push the clicked line as return destination. ← back (「← 戻る」) or `⌘[` (`def.back`) returns one step at a time (highlights clicked line). Lines in code column mean diff-column rows in diff list (hunk lines; `DefTarget.shown`); in by purpose and courses, head lines of that column's one file (preview file in by purpose; peeked file → peeked box → stop file in courses; reducer `defColumn`) plus base lines in its diffs. Keep stop, selected diff, and course progress (to return to reading). ← back sits in diff header (beside show all) in diff list/by purpose, and file bar (beside split / unified and full file) in courses. If analysis supports name navigation, show disabled when no return destination
  2. If absent from code column (unchanged file; in diff list, unchanged lines in changed files; in courses/by purpose, other files, including other stops' diffs), show full file in a right drawer (same form as unchanged-counterpart drawer; `components/DefDrawer.tsx`), scroll to definition and highlight. Header: no diff (also before change (base) (「変更前（base）」) for base), clicked name, path and line. `⇧R` or enlarge fills center (`rel.big` applies to an open definition drawer). Continue ⌘+clicking names inside drawer (drawer-local stack; ← previous definition (「← 前の定義」), `⌘[`); if destination is in code column, close drawer and go there. Esc/outside closes and returns to the diff being read. No viewed mark. Never open together with map or unchanged-counterpart drawer (latest stays)
  3. If library (jar/JDK), only show the definition of X is in a library (「`X` の定義はライブラリの中です」) and jar name at lower right. Do not display library code
  - Left-column (base) names go to base definitions. If in a file unchanged by the PR, show the same head location (identical content). If in a changed file, show full base text (`Analysis.baseFiles`)
  - Clear drawer and return destinations when code column changes (screen, course, stop, peeked item; in by purpose, preview file changes. `defMoved`). Preserve during scrolling within diff list
  - Query in two stages: names on visible-screen lines (head: full files, including changed files and unchanged call-related files; base: diff lines), then all names in destination files absent from diff column. Beyond that, retain full text only, without querying names. Below drawer: cannot navigate from names in this file (…) (「このファイルの名前からは移動できません（…）」) (do not silently hide the approximation; principle 2)
  - Do not extract names in summary deviation diffs or unchanged-counterpart drawer (opening from the latter would overlap the definition drawer; add separately if needed). No keyboard-only name selection (ondina code has no cursor)
- **Open in IDEA**: `jetbrains://<product>/navigate/reference?project=<name>&path=<repo相対パス>:<行>` (JetBrains Toolbox URL scheme). `product` and `project` use configuration `ide` (chapter 11; default `idea`; if no `project`, target repository directory name). Full relative path such as `src/main/kotlin/com/example/…`

### 3.7 Progress meter definitions

| Item | Definition |
|---|---|
| Attribution (「帰属」) | Hunks belonging to Hows / all hunks |
| Viewed (「閲覧」) | Viewed hunks / all hunks (follow-on group members count individually; viewing the representative does not mark members. Decided 2026-09-19) |
| Entanglement (「絡み」) | Number of hunks belonging to two or more Hows |
| Courses | Courses with all changed stops viewed / all courses (exclude outside courses; separate from complete button; calculated from viewed marks) |
| Specification | Requirements with corresponding courses (Hows) / all requirements (10.8. Program counts only existing Hows among LLM-selected mappings; 0/0 if no requirements) |

Right side of the course bar (3.3. 2026-10-22): that course's “checks: sentences whose supporting places are all marked no issues / sentences · no issues: stops / stops.” Both use viewed marks, separate from the complete button. Check sentences and places come from the LLM; the program counts (principle 3)

### 3.8 Unresolved UI issues

- **Review workspace** (2026-11-06): The general settings tab offers a destination (disabled / Herdr app / herdr terminal / orca), a maker (gct / let the destination app create it), an editable agent command, and an initial prompt. These settings live in `workspace` in `.ondina/ui.json` and apply across PRs. An unset destination disables the feature. The default maker is `app` and the default agent is `claude`. Empty agent and prompt strings mean no launch and no prompt, respectively. Only `{pr}`, `{title}`, `{branch}`, and `{url}` are replaced. Variable buttons insert at the cursor, with a live preview for sample PR #1234. The CLI supplies the default prompt and executable availability through `GET /api/workspace/tools`. Missing CLIs are indicated beside their options; options remain visible. gct follows the location, copy, and symlink settings in `.gct.toml`.
- Viewed is set only by pressing a button (decided 2026-09-19; not merely opening preview or scrolling). It is closer to no issues or “file-level Approve” than “actually viewed,” so do not set automatically. The exception is import-only diffs when the user turns on the setting 「import だけの差分を自動でチェック済みにする」 (`autoCheckImports`; 2026-11-07; see the next item)
- **Auto-check import-only diffs** (2026-11-07. `UiSettings.autoCheckImports`, off by default): one row in the General tab of settings (On / Off; note: 「ファイルの先頭の差分のうち、package と import の行だけが変わったものです。コースではそこに止まりません。」). When on, diffs with `Hunk.importsOnly` (5.2) are added to `ReviewState.viewed` and saved when an analysis is opened (`data.loaded`) and when the setting is turned on (for the open analysis too). They go through the same path as a manual check: when every diff of a file is viewed, GitHub Viewed is set (12.1; checks added on opening are sent too; local does not send). Turning the setting off does not remove checks already added. Course behavior is in 3.3
- Keyboard selection of memo lines (currently only clicking line-number “+” with a mouse; 2026-10-06)
- ~~Layout for collapsing the map when a core hunk is long on a course stop screen~~ → Resolved 2026-09-30. Map is a drawer (`M`). Since 2026-10-12, left guidance can also collapse with `B`
- Expand map bundle boxes within the map (currently jumps to by purpose and expands the bundle; 2026-09-25). Same for related bundle boxes (bundles absent from reading order): pressing goes to by purpose instead of peeking in the right column (2026-09-26; decide peeking together with in-map bundle expansion)
- Whether to exclude file-level symbols (`kind: 'file'`) from map and reading order (5 of 6 fixture instances duplicate declarations in the same file; currently shown)
- ~~How to show an overview across Hows~~ → Resolved 2026-09-30. Entry map tab (3.1)
- UI to expand and spot-check remaining follow-on group members — done (2026-09-19. Expanding a group row in by purpose lists every member individually; 3.2)
- Allow arbitrary base/head worktree locations from the UI (in the UI, not configuration; decided 2026-09-16) — done (2026-09-16. UI input (settings modal since 2026-09-30; previously rail bottom), saved by `serve` to `.ondina/ui.json`. `ondina analyze` reads it if no `--worktree-root`. Since 2026-10-23, only the language-server worktree location (analysis creates no worktrees; 5.1). When head advances, reanalyze from the row under the bar (3.0; 2026-10-13; previously reran `analyze` in CLI))
- LLM command (5.3) can also be set in UI (decided/implemented 2026-09-17). Same settings section saves `.ondina/ui.json` `llmCommand`; `ondina analyze` priority: UI > environment `ONDINA_LLM_COMMAND` > yaml `llm.command` > default. Takes effect on next `analyze`
- AI-review command (3.11) in the same settings section (2026-10-22). `.ondina/ui.json` `reviewCommand`. Separate from the analysis / ask-the-guide LLM command; specify an agent with tools (git, file reads). LLM-command heading changed to LLM command (analysis and ask the guide) (「LLM コマンド（解析と案内人に聞く）」). Explanation: run with sh -c per PR. Review instructions on stdin, JSON on stdout (same format as ondina comments add). Number passed in $ONDINA_PR, base/head in $ONDINA_BASE_SHA and $ONDINA_HEAD_SHA. If empty, hide “ask AI to review” (「PR ごとに sh -c で起動します。stdin にレビューの指示、stdout に JSON（ondina comments add と同じ形式）。番号は $ONDINA_PR、base と head は $ONDINA_BASE_SHA と $ONDINA_HEAD_SHA で渡します。空なら「AI にレビューを頼む」は出しません」); placeholder: tool-enabled agent command example (`claude -p --model opus --allowedTools "Bash(git diff:*),Bash(git show:*),Read,Grep,Glob"`). If empty, hide ask AI to review (「AI にレビューを頼む」) in PR list. Takes effect on next request
- Ticket retrieval (5.1) in the same settings section (2026-10-12). `UiSettings.ticket`: method (Jira / command), Jira URL, Jira email (Cloud only), retrieval command. Do not put tokens in settings; use Mac Keychain (`ondina-jira`; set with `ondina token`) or environment `ONDINA_TICKET_TOKEN`. Takes effect on next `analyze`
- All UI settings in one `.ondina/ui.json` (`UiSettings`), shared across PRs (2026-10-11; none need per-PR values, so save all together): auto-check import-only diffs (`autoCheckImports`; 3.3, 3.8; 2026-11-07), worktree location, LLM command, AI-review command (3.11; 2026-10-22), theme (3.5), split / unified (by purpose `b`, course stops `c`, diff list `d`), shortcut keys (`keymap`; 18.2; 2026-10-20), column widths (`panes`; left/right stop columns and diff list file column; 3.3; 2026-10-20), playback speed of the home map route (`mapSpeed`; 3.1; 2026-11-04). Per-PR `ReviewState` contains only review progress (viewed, memos, course progress). Do not read `ui` (theme, split / unified) in older states (removed on next save). Scope is per repository (`.ondina/` is in the repository)

### 3.9 Wrap-up

Goal: After finishing courses, show the remainder on one screen and hand off to approval. Indicate unviewed diffs and outside-course places without blocking approval (principle 8). Implemented 2026-10-03 (`packages/web/src/closing.ts`, `components/ClosingView.tsx`). Navigation and contents decided one question at a time with examples (based on demo's `screenClosing`).

Entry points: top-bar wrap-up (anytime; also shows return to course n if still in a course), last course summary's complete · to wrap-up, entry's to wrap-up after all courses complete. No key. `Esc` returns to the originating course if any, otherwise entry.

Screen (same arrangement as summary: main content left, drafts and approval right):
- Heading: wrap-up and PR title
- Three counts: courses marked complete n / N (complete-button presses; exclude outside courses. Top-bar meter courses counts courses with all stops viewed, a separate count), diffs marked no issues n / N (hunks), comments to send n (personal memos + accepted drafts; “—” if no memos and no undiscarded drafts. Changed from “accepted drafts n / m” on 2026-10-06)
- One line of pilot guidance (automatic): n diffs not viewed. Approval is always available (「見ていない差分 n。承認はいつでも押せる。」) (if all viewed: all diffs viewed. … (「差分は全部見た。…」))
- Diffs outside courses (when any): each place has dot, name, path, `+n −m`, no issues / unviewed, view (go to that outside-course stop). Below: view n places in order (「n か所を順に見る」) (retry if all viewed)
- Unviewed diffs: up to 12 hunks without no-issues marks, in diff list order (course-color circles, name, path, same rewrite (「同じ書き換え」) for members of identical rewrites, open = select hunk in diff list `diff.open`). Remaining: n more (「ほか n」)
- Right: personal memos (「自分のメモ」) and AI drafts boxes, one send button (below), Approve on GitHub `Enter` (`closing.approve`; only opens PR page in new tab; unavailable for analyses without PR numbers, with tooltip reason), to overview

Right column (moved from diff list's right column on 2026-10-03 to put sending in one place and reread immediately before approval. Added memo box on 2026-10-06; examples led to “two boxes, one send button”):
- **Personal memos** (send as is (「そのまま送る」)): Memos written on course stops or diff list (3.3 / 3.4). Each: `パス 行` (ranges: lines 8–11 (「8〜11 行目」); base: (base side) (「（base 側）」)), cannot send to GitHub, sent, open, body, edit (「編集」) (inline writing box), delete (「削除」). No acceptance step (self-written). Open goes where lines are visible (open item below)
- **AI drafts** (send only accepted drafts (「採用したものだけ送る」)): Below
- Comments received as JSON from external AI agents (Claude Code, CodeRabbit, etc.) appear as drafts (「下書き」) (`path:line`, source chip, body). Chapter 12
- `採用` / `編集` / `破棄`. Editing changes body in a text area; accept this text. Since 2026-10-13, the same drafts also appear below diff list and course lines (3.3); pressing anywhere changes the same draft. Edits in progress are shared too (`draftText`)
- Open (`comment.open`; same for personal memos and AI drafts) goes where lines are visible, in order: (1) if a line is in a diff list hunk, select that hunk and scroll to the memo line (`hunkAt` in `files.ts`; RIGHT = head-side, LEFT = base-side line number); (2) otherwise (far from changes), a course stop screen where the line is visible (`memoStop` in `memo.ts`; first stop whose initially visible 3-line context includes it); (3) if outside all stops' context, first stop in the same file, showing full file; (4) unchanged file (no stop passes through it): open the counterpart in the unchanged-counterpart drawer (keep screen; counterpart whose center is that line → counterpart whose excerpt contains it → first counterpart in the file. `relAt` in `rel.ts`; 2026-10-11); (5) old analysis without full files: open file in diff list. Also scroll to memo lines for (2)–(3) (distant full-file lines enter view). Scroll marker (`MemoUi.focus`) is one-shot; clear when screen, stop, or selected hunk changes (do not jump to an old memo on every return to diff list). Added (1)'s scroll and (2)–(3) on 2026-10-06 (previously outside-diff lines only opened the file's first hunk, leaving memos invisible)
- One send button below both boxes: `GitHub にコメントを送る（メモ n · 下書き m）`. Send personal memos and accepted drafts as one GitHub review. **The reviewer always owns the comments**. For analyses without PRs (`local`), button is `AI に返す（メモ n · 下書き m）`, returning to the AI that made the change (chapter 12; 2026-10-15). Memos/drafts on lines absent from GitHub's diff (written before 2026-10-22, or carried to a new version and now outside its diff) have cannot send to GitHub chips, are excluded from sending counts, and are not sent. Under the button: n memos/drafts on lines outside GitHub's diff will not be sent. GitHub cannot attach comments to those lines (「GitHub の差分に出ない行のメモ・下書き n 件は送りません。GitHub はこの行にコメントを付けられません」) (reviewer decides whether to remove or edit; never delete automatically). Old analyses without GitHub diff ranges: this analysis has no GitHub diff ranges. Rerun analyze to identify lines outside GitHub's diff (「この解析には GitHub の差分の範囲がありません。analyze を実行し直すと、GitHub の差分に出ない行を見分けられます」)

### 3.10 Ask the guide

Goal: Ask the guide (LLM) in place about things not understood while reading. Answers use diffs and analysis facts without judging correctness (principle 1). Also answer questions beyond code (term meanings, language/library mechanisms, etc.); for missing material, if the LLM command can use the internet, research and link pages (2026-10-15; sources below). For code absent from the material, the guide asks for files and strings to read, and serve reads them from the analyzed version and asks again. Answer "why" from the PR body, ticket, background, commit messages, and per-diff intents, and when none of them says, infer it from the material and label it "inferred" (2026-11-04 / 2026-11-11; 10.5). Implemented 2026-10-08 (`packages/web/src/ask.ts`, `components/AskDrawer.tsx` / `AskButton.tsx` / `StopColumn.tsx` (stop-screen right column; 2026-10-20), `packages/core/src/ask/`, `/api/ask` in `packages/cli/src/serve.ts`). Three decisions chosen one question at a time with examples (demo ask only existed on actual-code screen and answered just two prepared questions).

Decisions (2026-10-07–08):
1. **Ask on any screen**. The screen determines the topic: entry/wrap-up = whole PR; summary = that course (outside courses: places outside courses (「コースに入っていない場所」)); stop screen = that stop (guidance screen removed on 2026-10-12, so also the stop immediately after entry; since 2026-10-20, remains stop while peeking; outside courses: one diff); diff list = selected diff (otherwise file); by purpose = selected diff (otherwise whole PR). `askPlace` in `ask.ts`
2. **If lines are selected, ask about those lines**. Make memo inserts only the question into the asked-about lines' writing box (not the answer; send the reviewer's words). Implemented in the second PR on 2026-10-08 (`askLinesPlace` in `ask.ts`, `pickFor` / `mergeDraft` in `memo.ts`, reducer `askMemo`)
3. **Offer fixed-form suggestions** (select from place facts; automatic chip; do not let the LLM write suggestions; analysis stays unchanged)

Screen:
- Ask `Q` in each screen's heading row (entry: left of start; summary/wrap-up: right column; diff list: left of open in IDEA; by purpose: preview heading. Absent on stop screen, whose right column is the question area). Gray upper-right dot while waiting; accent dot when answer arrives while not viewed (unread; since 2026-10-20, tracked per place and clears when that conversation is visible. `AskUi.unread`)
- Drawer from right (420px). Does not cover top bar or dim background (code remains visible while reading answers; pressing citations visibly moves code). Instead of overlay, shift screen left while open (overlay hid no issues · next and diff list right column; verified on the actual UI on 2026-10-08. Examples also used side-by-side layout). Close: `Esc` / `Q` / close. Below 1180px, overlay screen and shrink to `clamp(320px,50vw,420px)` (shifting left leaves too little; course code was 60px in a 760px window. 2026-10-13; screen width in 3.0)
- About below heading (`askTitle`, such as `domain.Order（コース 3 の 1 か所目）`). Drawer **stays at the place where opened** (citation navigation does not change conversation; closing/reopening uses current place)
- **Right column is the question area on stop screens** (2026-10-20; three columns in 3.3. Working examples chose guidance in conversation; repeated questions push guidance upward, so added to top / to bottom): No drawer (close on entering stop screen; do not automatically reopen on summary). About **follows the current stop** (on course/stop/screen changes, set current stop and show its conversation; peeking does not change it. Writing-box ask and × whole place still change it; reducer postprocessing `followStop`). First conversation message is pilot guidance (3.3); empty conversation shows you can ask about things you do not understand, including things beyond code (「分からないことを聞けます。コードの外のことも聞けます。」) and common questions (「よく聞くこと」). `Q` focuses right input (expands if collapsed; `AskUi.focusN`). `Esc` in input only blurs. Do not send while collapsed. If an answer arrives at another place while unseen, above input show an answer to a question elsewhere has arrived (place name) (「別の場所の問いの答えが届いています（場所の名前）」) (same in drawer; no navigation button, since top circles navigate). Conversation scroll: top on stop change (read guidance first); bottom when answer/wait/failure appears at same place. If overflow, small round to top / to bottom buttons at lower right (`ask.top` / `ask.bottom`; disable the side already reached; no keys)
- Conversations per place (keys `pr` / `course:3` / `symbol:<id>` / `hunk:<id>` / `file:<path>`; same symbol shares conversation with or without course). Follow-up questions include the last 3 question/answer pairs at that place as material
- Empty conversation: three lines, you can ask about things you do not understand, including things beyond code; answers use diffs and analysis facts (「答えは差分と解析の事実から書きます。」); missing details are researched online or answered from AI knowledge (「足りないことは、ネットで調べるか AI の知識で答えます。」), then common-question suggestions (automatic chip). Press to send immediately. `askSuggestions`: symbol/diff = how did it behave before the change? (「変更前はどう動いていた？」) (changed places) / what does this do? (「ここは何をしている？」) (unchanged places) / what is being checked? (「何を確かめている？」) (tests); how does this differ from other places with the same rewrite? (「同じ書き換えのほかの場所と何が違う？」) (deviations); where is this called from? (「ここを呼んでいる所は？」) (incoming call edges); which tests check this? (「どのテストが確かめている？」) (tests present); how does this connect to the next place? (「次の場所とどうつながる？」) (next course stop exists). File = what changed in this file? (「このファイルで変わったことは？」). Course = where does behavior change in this course? (「このコースで振る舞いが変わる所は？」), where does this course start? (「このコースはどこから始まる？」). Whole PR = where does behavior change? (「振る舞いが変わる所は？」), where does behavior stay the same? (「振る舞いが変わらない所は？」), what are the n places outside courses? (「コースに入っていない n か所は何？」)
- Multiline input; send with `⌘⏎` / `Ctrl⏎` (not Enter used to confirm Japanese input; same as memo save). Draft survives closing
- **Sources (「出どころ」)** (2026-10-15. With codex exec, it accepted only code questions, answered “I don't know” to “what is this?” and did not research online. Cause: instructions “answer only from facts in the material” and “do not use tools.” Chose footnotes among three examples (line endings / grouped by source / footnotes)): Internet-researched facts end with green `[1]` (press to open page); researched pages (「調べたページ」) below answer (`[1] ページの題 kotlinlang.org ↗`; once per page; program derives site from URL; opens new tab; `askWebRefs` in `ask.ts`). Facts without line citations or pages (LLM prior knowledge) get no source (「出典なし」) chips. ondina cannot verify that the page says this (open and read). Default command (`claude -p --tools ""`) cannot use internet, so beyond-code answers have no source. codex (verified on 0.162.1) researches with plain `codex exec`
- Answers are LLM prose, with dotted border and AI chip (principle 4). Opening (lead) and ending (tail) break one sentence per line at punctuation; facts in bullet points. Fact line citations have blue underlines (`Order.kt 13`; base: base side Order.kt 6 (「base 側 Order.kt 6」); range: 8–11 (「8〜11」); `citeLabel`). If LLM-written locations are absent from material, remove them and show cannot verify location (「場所を確かめられない」) (do not silently hide failed approximations)
- **Press a line citation** (`ask.cite`): On stop screen, stay if line is visible at current stop (full file if outside 3-line context; stop peeking if needed). Other lines: if full file exists, peek without moving stops (3.3; 2026-10-20, since moving changes right-column conversation and loses the answer being read). Old analyses without full text and outside stop screens use wrap-up open routing (current-course stop showing line → diff list hunk → visible stop → full file → unchanged-counterpart drawer for unchanged files). Briefly flash destination line, leave marker left of line number (`AppState.focusLine`; flash again per press; clear on screen/stop/selected-hunk change). Same function as `comment.open` (reducer `openLine`). If this route leaves stop screen, open drawer and preserve the same conversation
- While waiting: waiting for answer · n seconds (「答えを待っています · n 秒」) and stop (「止める」) under question bubble. Can close and continue reading. Only one pending question at a time (disable send (「送る」) and suggestions). Stopping returns question to input; serve terminates the whole LLM process group
- Failure (missing command, exit code ≠ 0, over 5 minutes (including the rounds that ask again), unreadable JSON, no answer after reading twice): could not write an answer with the LLM (「LLM で答えを書けませんでした。」), reason, retry, edit LLM command (「LLM コマンドを直す」) (settings modal, same as entry bar). Do not save failed or stopped questions
- **Ask about lines** (decision 2): Ask in the writing box opened by selecting lines with “+” (new-writing boxes only). Opens drawer about selected lines (changes about if already open; on stop screen, changes right-column about and focuses input; `ask.lines`). `Q` does the same outside inputs. About: `Order.kt 8〜11 行目（domain.Order）` (same line notation as memo heading). On stop screen add lines to current place (lines of another peeked file follow diff list rules); diff list uses diff if both endpoints share one, otherwise file. Far-right × whole place (「× 場所全体にする」) (`ask.whole`) clears lines. No key label on writing-box ask (Q types a character inside writing box; differs from example)
- Separate conversation per line range (key `symbol:<id>#h8-h11`; endpoint side initials (head/base) and line numbers). Suggestions: if selected lines include added/removed lines, how did it behave before the change? and what does this do?; otherwise only what does this do? (line ranges lack caller/test-edge information)
- **Make memo** (`ask.memo`): Only for answers about code places (symbol/diff/file); not whole PR or course (memos attach to lines). For answers about lines, insert question into those lines' writing box. If current selection matches asked-about lines, use that box; if visible on current screen, reselect there. Otherwise follow wrap-up open routing (`openLine`) to a place showing them, reselect and highlight (calculate `MemoPos.order` using destination screen's counting). Preserve draft and append after newline (do not append duplicates; do not carry previous screen's draft to a destination screen). Under answer, change to inserted into the writing box for lines 8–11 (「8〜11 行目の書く枠に入れた」); returns to make memo when that box closes (save/cancel/move)
- Make memo for questions without lines, or when asked-about lines cannot be shown (outside-diff lines in old analyses without full text): under answer, select memo lines using “+” at line numbers (「メモを付ける行を、行番号の「+」で選んでください。」) and cancel. Insert question into the next “+”-selected lines' writing box (do not select lines automatically). Stay on screen (user may want lines after following an answer citation). Clear on drawer close, cancel, or next send (avoid inserting text unexpectedly when selecting lines with drawer closed)
- Call LLM only on send (suggestion/retry) presses; once per question (up to 3 times when the guide asks for code to read, because serve reads it and asks again. 2026-11-04; 10.5), never during analysis. Serve appends answers to `.ondina/<key>/ask/<headSha>.json` (separate from `ReviewState`, avoiding writes racing with UI `PUT /api/state`). Conversations survive reload. Input/output in 10.5

### 3.11 PR list (2026-10-22)

- **Open for review** (2026-11-06): The workspace row on each PR card and detail drawer prepares a worktree on the PR head branch, registers it in the selected app, and starts the configured agent with the filled prompt. It does not invent a new branch name. While running, it displays the steps chosen at the start and checks completed steps. Completion shows the app name and an open button; failure shows a reason and retry. With no destination configured, cards omit the row and the drawer links to settings. The `pr.workspace` command sends the PR number, branch, title, and URL to `POST /api/workspace/:n`. A running job for that PR returns 409 with its current state. `GET /api/workspace` returns the latest jobs, polled every second while running. `.ondina/workspaces.json` stores app, path, branch, and opening time and restores the display after a serve restart. If the recorded worktree still exists, pressing again focuses it without creating another worktree or agent (orca launches one if no terminal remains). Branches absent from origin, including fork PR branches, are currently unsupported. To prevent Orca from creating a suffixed branch, app creation stops with an explanation if the local PR branch differs from origin.
Lists open PRs, lets users choose where to start reading, and starts analysis in place. Can also ask an AI agent to review (2026-10-22; ask AI to review below). Previously required CLI `analyze <n>` (some environments lack copy/paste). Group PRs by author; show labels, review status, and analysis state (complete / not done / running / updated since analysis); analyze (「解析する」) runs through worktree creation, pull, and analysis. Compared five examples and chose “author × review status.” `components/PrListView.tsx`; `prs.ts` determines content

```
┌ [すべて | あなたに依頼]  ☑ Draft も出す                        GitHub から 1 分前に取得 [更新] ┐
├──────────┬─────────────────┬─────────────────┬─────────────────┤
│          │ ○ 誰も見ていない 3 │ ● コメントあり 2   │ ● 承認あり 1       │
│          │ レビューもコメントもまだ│ コメントか修正の依頼がある│ 1 人以上が承認した   │
├──────────┼─────────────────┼─────────────────┼─────────────────┤
│ (T) yamada │ ┌ #8 ───────────┐ │ ┌ #42 修正の依頼 ─┐ │                 │
│ 2 件       │ │ 題名 / ラベル / 人 │ │ │ …              │ │                 │
│          │ │ 解析 ○ 未解析 [解析する]│ │ │ 解析 ● 解析済み [開く]│ │                 │
└──────────┴─────────────────┴─────────────────┴─────────────────┘
```

- **Rows and columns**: Rows = authors, columns = review status. At least one approval: approved (「承認あり」) (ignore required-approval count); comments or change requests: has comments (「コメントあり」); neither (including requests only): no one has reviewed (「誰も見ていない」). Change requests go under has comments, distinguished by red changes requested (「修正の依頼」) on cards. Authors sorted by latest update; your PRs last. Within cells, newest updates first. Column headings have counts and one-line explanations, sticky at top. Grouping is a UI concern, hence pure web functions (`reviewColumn` / `prRows`). CLI returns only per-person facts (5.5)
- **Cards**: `#番号`, Draft, changes requested, your marker (requested from you (「あなたに依頼」), you: approved (「あなた: 承認済み」), etc.; `youOf`), title, labels (GitHub colors), reviewers (circle + login + approval/change request/comment/not reviewed; team requests use team names), +added −removed, file count, updated ○○ ago, CI passed / failed / running, comment count, analysis row
- **Analysis row** (`prAnalysis`; determined from web's analysis list `GET /api/analyses` and serve's queue; no new API): Not analyzed (「未解析」): analyze; queued: n ahead (「前に n 件」); analyzing (spinner + elapsed m:ss + latest progress line; same as row under bar); analyzed (「解析済み」) (analysis exists for current PR head): open; updated since analysis (「解析後に更新あり」) (old-head analysis only): reanalyze and open previous analysis (「前の解析を開く」); could not analyze (「解析できませんでした」) (reason + retry; also open if previous analysis exists). Do not show example's “4/5” (stage number). Stage count varies with LLM, first-run structure inference, and tickets; it is not fixed
- **Analyze**: Enqueue with serve (5.5). One runs at a time; append after queued work if busy. Same queue as reanalyze in the row under the bar (same action). First PR analysis: `analyze <n>`; reanalyze/retry reuse previous analysis version's arguments (same as bar row; preserve ticket specified by `--ticket`). On press, show running or queued using serve's same rules, then replace with serve's response. While queued/running work exists, poll serve queue each second; reload analysis list if any PR finishes
- **Open**: Load analysis and arrive at overview (3.1) (even another version of the same PR goes to entry when opened from PR list)
- **Top**: all / requested from you (「すべて / あなたに依頼」) (only PRs requested personally from you; exclude team requests), include Draft (「Draft も出す」); right side: retrieved from GitHub n minutes ago (「GitHub から n 分前に取得」) and refresh (「更新」). Retrieval failure preserves previous list and shows reason
- **When to refresh**: Opening PR list (including `P`), refresh, returning to window while PR list is open. No time-based throttling (would require a reducer clock)
- Loading: retrieving PR list from GitHub (「GitHub から PR の一覧を取得しています」); failure: could not retrieve PR list from GitHub (「GitHub から PR の一覧を取得できませんでした。」), reason, retry; zero: no open PRs (「開いている PR はありません。」); zero after filtering: no PRs match conditions (「条件に合う PR はありません。」); 101+: showing only the 100 most recently updated (「更新が新しい 100 件だけを出しています。」)
- Disable keys affecting open-analysis diffs (`j` `k` `v` `[` `]` `G I`) in PR list (do not mark invisible diffs). `Esc` only closes PR details; does nothing if closed
- **PR details** (2026-10-22; shape decided with examples): Press a card (click, or focus and `Enter`) to open a right drawer. Same form as course map drawer (from right, width `min(62vw,1100px)`, slides in only on opening), below top bar. Do not dim background; left table stays clickable. Drawer covers right table columns, so allow horizontal table scrolling while open (author column sticks left). Scroll opened card left of drawer to show it; accent border. Press another card to switch contents; press same again to close. Buttons inside cards (analyze/open, etc.) perform only their own action. Close with `Esc`, close, or leaving PR list. Also close if PR disappears from refreshed list (closed/merged). `components/PrDetailDrawer.tsx` (shared card components in `PrParts.tsx`)
  - Top: `#番号`, Draft, changes requested, your marker (same as card); right: open on GitHub ↗ (「GitHub で開く ↗」) (new tab), close Esc (「閉じる Esc」); title; author circle/login, `head → base`, updated ○○ ago, labels; analysis row (same component and buttons as card). Draw from list facts (`OpenPr`), so appears before details arrive
  - Tabs: explain, reviews and comments n (「レビューとコメント n」), changed files n (「変更したファイル n」), checks n (「チェック n」). Start on explain every time (counts appear after details arrive)
  - **Explain**: PR body as Markdown (same `BodyMarkdown` rendering as entry's author explanation; no markers/code colors). If empty: no description written (「説明は書かれていません。」)
  - **Reviews and comments (「レビューとコメント」)**: Oldest first in GitHub timeline. Each = circle + login + kind (approval / changes requested / review comment / dismissed review / comment) + ○○ ago (hover for datetime) + body (Markdown). Reviews with line comments show `path:行` and body below (first 10; n more (「ほか n 件」) beyond that; outdated lines use line when written). Added commits appear as one added n commits (「コミットを n 件追加」) row; combine consecutive additions (even across people). Include author/bots as facts (unlike per-person list status). Retrieve only latest 100; if older entries exist: n older entries not shown. View on GitHub (「古い n 件は出していません。GitHub で見られます。」). Count excludes commit additions. Zero: no reviews or comments yet (「まだレビューもコメントもありません。」)
  - **Changed files (「変更したファイル」)**: Paths and +/− (GitHub order). Beyond 100: n more
  - **Checks (「チェック」)**: Latest commit's checks. Name, duration (description for StatusContext), result (success / failure / running / skipped / cancelled / neutral / action required / stale). Beyond 50: n more. Zero: no checks (「チェックはありません。」)
  - When to retrieve: every open (remember fetched content per number; keep visible during refresh, with retrieving (「取得しています」) to right of tabs), list refresh and window return (only open PR), retry. First load: retrieving PR details from GitHub (「GitHub から PR の詳細を取得しています」); failure: could not retrieve PR details from GitHub (「GitHub から PR の詳細を取得できませんでした。」), reason, retry (retain previous content below if any)
  - Reducer state: `prs.detail` (`{ number, tab }` or null) and `prs.details` (number → `{ data, loading, error }`; not saved). Commands: `pr.detail` / `pr.detail.tab` / `pr.detail.refresh` / `pr.github` / `prDetail.loaded` / `prDetail.failed`; effect: `fetchPrDetail`
- **Ask AI to review** (2026-10-22. Added LLM-agent review alongside analysis. Agent command is configured separately from analysis / ask the guide. Shape: example cards' AI row and settings field): AI row under analysis rows in cards and drawer (`prAiReview`; determined by serve AI-review jobs `GET /api/review` and settings; pressing only emits a command). Agent findings become AI drafts; reviewer chooses accept/edit/discard in wrap-up (chapter 12; ondina does not judge correctness; principle 1)
  - Not requested: ask AI to review; send serve the current PR head (list's `headSha`)
  - Waiting for analysis: starts when analysis finishes (「解析が終わったら始めます」) + stop. If current-head analysis is missing or this PR's analysis is queued/running, serve first enqueues analysis (if absent; reuse previous-head arguments if any, preserving `--ticket`) and waits for completion. Analysis row becomes queued (「順番待ち」) / analyzing (「解析中」)
  - Reviewing (spinner + elapsed m:ss) + stop
  - Finished: n drafts (「下書き n 件」) + view. n counts AI drafts (undecided, editing, accepted) in the reviewed head's state; serve recounts per request (changes when accepted/discarded in wrap-up). If comments on lines absent from GitHub diff exist, below: n excluded because their lines are absent from GitHub's diff (「n 件は GitHub の差分に出ない行なので入れませんでした」)
  - Updated since review (reviewed head differs from current PR head): n drafts · updated since review (「下書き n 件 · レビュー後に更新あり」) + request again (「頼み直す」) (current head)
  - Failed: could not review (「レビューできませんでした」) + reason (5.5) + retry
  - Stopped: stopped (「止めました」) + retry (state absent from examples; returning stopped jobs to not-requested would hide that they were stopped)
  - Empty command: omit card row; only in drawer show enter an AI-review command in settings to request from here (「設定で AI レビューのコマンドを入れると、ここから頼めます。」) and open settings (「設定を開く」). Even with empty command, show if a job exists for that PR (to stop running reviews)
- **View**: Load the reviewed head's analysis and arrive at wrap-up (3.9) (`pr.drafts`; reload even current analysis to list drafts)
- Top-bar running jobs (heading: running jobs) shows AI reviews running n (number reviewing; exclude analysis-waiting jobs, whose analyses show as analyzing/queued)
- While analysis or AI review runs, poll both serve's analysis queue and AI reviews each second (AI review may enqueue analysis). Also refresh AI reviews when fetching PR list (align draft counts with wrap-up accept/discard). When current analysis's review finishes, reload its state and list drafts (`loadComments` → `comments.arrived`)
- Reducer state: `reviews` (`ReviewJob[]`; failures to request serve persist until serve knows that PR; same `mergeJobs` as analysis). Commands: `pr.review` / `pr.review.stop` / `pr.drafts` / `review.jobs` / `review.startFailed` / `ui.reviewCommand`; effects: `startReview` / `stopReview` / `loadComments` and `view` in `loadAnalysis`
- Actual verification (2026-10-22): With fake gh and a fake review command (waits a few seconds, emits 3 JSON comments; one on a line in a file absent from diff), used headless Chrome through: enter settings → request on unanalyzed PR (analyzing and waiting for analysis) → reviewing (top-bar AI reviews running 1 (「AI レビュー中 1」)) → 2 drafts (「下書き 2 件」) and 1 excluded (…) (「1 件は … 入れませんでした」) → view shows 2 in wrap-up → request from another PR's drawer and stop during review

---

## 4. Data model

Project one model into each screen (entry, by purpose, courses, diff list). Shown in TypeScript (implementation language in chapter 6).

```ts
type Layer = string;                    // 設定ファイルで定義した名前。"API" | "Application" | "Domain" | "Infrastructure" | "Tests" など
type Kind = 'core' | 'ripple' | 'test' | 'none';   // 核 / 追従 / テスト / 未帰属
type Provenance = 'ast' | 'config' | 'lsp' | 'llm'; // 事実か推測かを表示で分けるために必ず持つ
type SymbolKind = 'class' | 'object' | 'companion' | 'function' | 'property' | 'constructor' | 'init' | 'typealias' | 'enumEntry' | 'file';  // 宣言の種別（5.2 の 1）

interface Hunk {
  id: string;            // sha1(path + '#' + symbolFqName)。push後も安定。閲覧状態のキー
  path: string;          // repo相対
  lang: 'kotlin' | 'sql' | string;
  symbol: string;        // 表示用。"applyDiscount()" / "OrderResponse"
  symbolFqName: string;  // com.example.order.domain.Order.applyDiscount。ファイル hunk なら path
  symbolKind: SymbolKind;
  kind: Kind;
  kindProvenance: Provenance;
  hows: number[];        // 所属How。2つ以上なら絡み、0なら未帰属
  n: number;             // メーター上の重み。全 hunk で 1（追従グループのメンバーも 1 件ずつ。3.7）
  lnOld: number; lnNew: number;
  lines: string[];       // 1文字目が ' ' | '+' | '-' の統合形式
  importsOnly?: true;    // on a Kotlin file hunk whose changed lines are only package / import / blank (5.2; 2026-11-07). Absent otherwise
  tokens?: TokenLine[];  // ハイライト済みトークン（7章）
  group?: RippleGroup;   // kind === 'ripple' のときのみ
}

interface RippleGroup {
  pattern: string;               // 核から導出。"getDiscount() → discount"
  derivedFrom: { coreHunkId: string; removed: string[]; added: string[] };
  uniform: 'ast' | 'llm';        // 表示: 一様（AST判定）/ たぶん一様（LLM判定）
  representative: string;        // メンバーhunk id
  members: string[];             // 同型と判定した hunk id
  deviations: string[];          // パターン外の hunk id
  distance: Record<string, number>; // 逸脱ごとの、代表の形との距離（単語単位の編集距離。8章）
  near: string[];                // deviations のうち距離が閾値（2）以下。単語列の比較では決めきれない場所
}

interface How {
  id: number;            // 依存順 = 表示順。再実行時は前回のidを維持
  title: string;         // 命令形・短く
  why: string;           // Aのどれに効くか1行
  specIds: string[];     // この How が対応する要求（Spec.id）。specs から逆に引く（10.8）
}

interface Symbol {
  id: string;            // sha1(path + '#' + fqName)。hunk を持つシンボルは Hunk.id と同じ式・同じ値
  fqName: string; path: string;
  layer?: Layer;         // 設定の glob か、構成の推定（10.6）の規則に当たらなければ欄ごと無い。当たらないファイルは LLM が推定する（10.2 の layerGuesses）
  layerProvenance?: Provenance;   // layer があるときだけ。設定の glob 由来なら 'config'、構成の推定と LLM の推定なら 'llm'（画面は「（推定）」）
  kind: SymbolKind;      // Hunk.symbolKind と同じ 10 種
  changed: boolean;      // false なら差分の無い相手（辺の相手として LSP から足したシンボル）。停留所にはしない。地図の「差分なし n か所」と引き出しに出す（3.3。2026-10-11）
  hunkId?: string;
  testCount?: number;    // そのシンボルを呼ぶテスト関数の数（5.2 の 7）。欄が無ければ未計測
  edgesMeasured?: boolean;  // 呼び出し関係を測れたか（9.6）。false = LSP が答えなかった。欄が無ければ未計測
}

interface Edge {
  from: string; to: string;               // Symbol.id
  kind: 'call' | 'schema' | 'test' | 'reference';  // reference = 名前を書いた・値を読んだ（textDocument/references。両端が変更シンボルのものだけ。9.2）
  n?: number;                             // reference だけ: 参照した箇所の数（地図で ×22 と出す）
  types?: { in?: string; out?: string };  // 辺ラベル。取れた側だけ入る（引数なし・戻り型なしの側は欄ごと無い。9.3）
  lines?: number[];                       // call / test だけ: 呼んでいる行（呼ぶ側のファイルの head。1 始まり。昇順・重複なし。callHierarchy の fromRanges の開始行。同じ両端の記録の行は合わせる）。読む順と「16 行で呼ぶ」の材料（2026-10-22）。欄が無い = 位置の無い記録・言語サーバーが返さなかった・古い解析
  crossesLayers: boolean;                 // 設定の allowedDependencies に反する。事実として描くだけ
  provenance: 'lsp' | 'llm';
}

interface Tour {                          // プログラムが call の辺を辿って組む（2026-09-24 決定。LLM に経路を書かせない。10.2）。差分の無いシンボルは並びを決めるのに使うが、ステップにはしない（2026-10-11）
  howId: number;
  change?: { before?: string; after?: string };  // 変わること（読む前の案内）。2 回目の LLM の文（5.3 / 10.4。2026-10-22）。出どころは常に llm なので provenance の欄は持たない。欄が無い = 画面は自動の文。片方だけのこともある
  checks?: TourCheck[];                   // 確かめること（読む前の案内と停留所の右の列）。2 回目の LLM が書き、場所はプログラムが確かめた物だけ残す（5.4 の 9）。番号は並びの順
  steps: Array<{ symbolId: string; hunkId?: string; call: string; role?: string; guide?: string; say?: string }>;  // call = 呼ばれ方 "ApplyDiscountUseCase.execute → OrderRepositoryImpl.save(Order)"。始点と辺に乗らない hunk は ''。role = この場所の 1〜2 文（2 回目の LLM）
  intro?: string;                         // 2026-10-22 より前の解析だけ: コースの紹介。steps[].guide / say も同じく古い解析だけ（なぜここかの 1 行・何を変えたかの 1 行）
}

interface TourCheck {
  text: string;                           // 確かめることの 1 文（観測。句点なし）
  at: Array<{ symbolId: string; lines: number[]; say?: string }>;  // 根拠の場所。steps の順。lines はその停留所の hunk の head 側（文脈か追加）の行番号。say = そこで見るもの
}

interface Ticket {                          // --ticket で指定したか、題・ブランチ名・本文から LLM が選んだチケット（3.1 / 5.1。2026-10-12）。中身は原文のまま。LLM を通さない
  ref: string;                              // --ticket に渡した値（番号・URL・ファイル）
  source: 'jira' | 'command' | 'file';      // 取り方
  at: string;                               // 取った（取ろうとした）時刻。ISO 8601
  key?: string; url?: string;               // 番号とチケットのページ。ファイルには無い
  title?: string; kind?: string; status?: string;  // 題・種類（ストーリーなど）・状態（進行中など）。取れたものだけ
  body?: string; format?: 'html' | 'markdown' | 'text';  // 本文と書式。html = Jira が直した HTML、text = 改行を保った文字
  error?: string;                           // 取れなかった理由。あれば ref / source / at / key / url 以外は無い
}
interface TicketSearch {                    // --ticket が無いときに題・ブランチ名・本文から探した記録（5.1 / 10.7。2026-10-12）
  candidates: TicketCandidate[];            // プログラムが拾った候補（番号の形と URL）。出てきた順（題 → ブランチ名 → 本文）。最大 20
  chosen?: string;                          // LLM が選んだ候補の ref（= pr.ticket.ref）。出どころは常に llm（推定）。無い = 選んでいない
  llm?: LlmRun;                             // 選ぶ LLM の記録。無い = 候補が無くて呼ばなかった。ok で chosen が無い = どれでもないと答えた
}
interface TicketCandidate {
  ref: string;                              // 取りに行く値。同じ番号の URL があれば URL（Jira の場所の設定が要らない）、無ければ番号
  label: string;                            // 画面の名前。番号。番号の無い URL は URL
  places: Array<{ in: 'title' | 'branch' | 'body'; line?: number; text: string; start: number; end: number }>;  // 見つけた場所。text はその行、start / end は拾った文字列の位置
}
interface Project {                         // どんな案件の一部か（Analysis.background.project。3.1 / 5.1。2026-10-29）。チケットを Jira から取れて、親があるときだけ
  parent?: ParentTicket;                    // 親チケット。無い = 親を調べられなかった（errors に理由）
  why?: string[];                           // 親の本文から要求の抜き出しが写した目的の文（10.8）。本文にある物だけ
  children?: string[];                      // 同じ親の下のチケットの番号（このチケットを含む。番号順）
  searched?: string[]; skipped?: number;    // PR を探した番号（親 → このチケット → 残りの子。30 個まで）と、探さなかった数
  repo?: string; prs?: ProjectPr[];         // この PR のリポジトリ（owner/name）と同じ案件の PR（この PR のリポジトリが先、その中は番号順）。探せなければ無い
  errors?: string[];                        // 取れなかった物の理由（画面にそのまま出す）
}
interface ParentTicket { key: string; url: string; via: 'parent' | 'epic'; title?: string; kind?: string; status?: string; body?: string; format?: 'html' | 'text' }  // 中身は原文のまま
interface ProjectPr { repo: string; number: number; title: string; url: string; state: 'open' | 'draft' | 'merged' | 'closed'; keys: string[]; self?: true }  // keys = 含んでいた番号。self = 同じリポジトリで同じ番号か同じブランチ名
interface Spec {
  id: string; text: string;             // s1, s2 …; wording and mapping inferred by the LLM
  hunkIds: string[];                     // LLM-selected, program-verified; empty = no corresponding diff found
  hows: number[];                        // How IDs derived from hunkIds, ascending
}
type Chapter =
  | { kind: 'spec'; specId: string; hunkIds: string[] } // Reading order; empty = no corresponding diff found
  | { kind: 'follow'; hunkIds: string[] }               // Ripple members following a core change
  | { kind: 'extra'; hunkIds: string[] };               // Diffs matching no requirement
interface CommitNote { sha: string; title: string; body: string }  // non-merge commits, oldest first
type IntentSource = 'pr-body' | 'ticket' | 'parent' | 'commit';
type IntentNote =
  | { kind: 'written'; text: string; from: IntentSource; quote: string; commit?: string } // commit is the verified full sha
  | { kind: 'guess'; text: string }   // provenance: llm
  | { kind: 'none'; text: string };   // provenance: llm
interface HunkIntent { hunkId: string; notes: IntentNote[]; ask?: string } // ask is a draft question to the author
interface Claim { text: string; backed: boolean; reason?: string } // 作者説明の各文
interface Summary { problem: string; approach: string[] | string; untouched: string[] | string; beforeAfter: Array<{target: string; before: string; after: string}> }  // 2 回目の LLM（10.4）。approach は文（2026-10-10 まではリスト）、untouched はリスト。古い解析は文字列

interface CommentDraft {
  label?: 'ask' | 'imo';  // Reviewer question / opinion; absent for older memos. UI metadata, never sent to GitHub.
  id: string; path: string; line: number; side: 'LEFT' | 'RIGHT';   // 範囲なら line / side は最後の行
  startLine?: number; startSide?: 'LEFT' | 'RIGHT';                // 範囲の始まり（GitHub の start_line / start_side）。1 行なら無い
  body: string; source: string;            // "reviewer"（自分のメモ。3.3。id は m1, m2, …）| "claude-code" | "coderabbit" | …
  note?: string;                           // AI drafts only. An explanation for the reviewer (why the claim holds). Never sent to GitHub (chapter 12, 2026-11-04)
  state: 'draft' | 'edit' | 'adopted' | 'discarded' | 'sent';  // sent = GitHub に送った後（12章）。再送しない。メモは保存した時点で adopted
}

interface ReviewState {                      // レビュアーのローカル状態。PR番号 + headSha をキーに永続化（serve が .ondina/<key>/state/<headSha>.json に書く）。その PR の進捗だけ（画面の設定は UiSettings。2026-10-11）
  viewed: string[];                          // Hunk.id。JSON に書くので配列（実装は packages/model）
  comments: CommentDraft[];
  course?: { done: string[]; completed: number[] };  // コースの進み具合（3.3）。done = 2026-10-11 より前の差分の無い停留所の問題なし "<コース id>:<Symbol.id>"（今は付けない）、completed = 完了にしたコース id。古い state には無い
  carried?: {                                // 前のバージョンから引き継いで作った state の記録（3.0。2026-10-13）。新しいバージョンを初めて開いたときに serve が書く
    from: string;                            // 前のバージョンの headSha
    reset: string[];                         // 「問題なし」だったが差分が変わったので未確認に戻した Hunk.id
    coursesReset: number[];                  // 完了だったが未完了に戻したコース id
    left: string[];                          // 移せず前のバージョンに残したメモ・下書きの id（12 章）
    dismissed?: true;                        // 帯の下の 1 行を閉じた
  };
}

interface FileText { path: string; tokens: TokenLine[] }  // head 側の全文（3.3 の停留所の画面）。行の文字列はトークンの連結

// 案内人に聞く（3.10 / 10.5。2026-10-08）。会話は ReviewState とは別に .ondina/<key>/ask/<headSha>.json（serve が答えを受け取った時点で足す）
interface AskLines { path: string; start: { side: 'head'|'base'; line: number }; end: { side: 'head'|'base'; line: number } }  // 「+」で選んだ行。start / end は画面の上からの順
type AskPlace = { kind: 'pr' } | { kind: 'course'; courseId: number } | { kind: 'symbol'; symbolId: string; courseId?: number; lines?: AskLines } | { kind: 'hunk'; hunkId: string; lines?: AskLines } | { kind: 'file'; path: string; lines?: AskLines };
interface AskCite { path: string; side: 'head'|'base'; line: number; endLine?: number }
interface AskAnswer { lead: string; items: Array<{ text: string; cite?: AskCite; unverified?: true }>; tail?: string }  // unverified = LLM が書いた場所が材料に無かった
interface AskEntry { id: string; key: string; place: AskPlace; question: string; answer: AskAnswer; command: string; ms: number; at: string }  // key = 場所の鍵（web が作る）
interface AskLog { entries: AskEntry[] }     // 古い順。失敗と止めた問いは入らない

interface Analysis {                         // パイプラインの成果物。JSON 1ファイル。headSha をキーにキャッシュ
  pr: { number: number; title: string; base: string; head: string; headSha: string; baseSha: string; body?: string; bodyCode?: BodyCode[]; ticket?: Ticket; ticketSearch?: TicketSearch };  // body は作者の説明の原文（3.1。2026-09-19）。bodyCode は本文のコードブロックの色（{ lang; code; tokens: TokenLine[] }。2026-10-10）。ticket は --ticket で指定したか題・ブランチ名・本文から LLM が選んだチケット（5.1。2026-10-12）。欄が無い = 指定も選んだものも無い。ticketSearch は --ticket が無いときに探した記録（欄が無い = --ticket の解析か古い解析）
  hunks: Hunk[]; hows: How[]; symbols: Symbol[]; edges: Edge[]; edgesBase?: Edge[]; tours: Tour[];  // edgesBase is the call edges before the change (base) (9.2. 2026-11-05). No field = an old analysis or one without a language server
  specs: Spec[]; claims: Claim[]; summary: Summary;
  chapters?: Chapter[];  // Program-built when a ticket yields requirements (10.8)
  intents?: HunkIntent[]; commits?: CommitNote[];  // omitted when empty (10.9)
  config: AnalysisConfig;                    // ondina.config.yaml の解析に効く部分（11章）。無ければ既定値
  llm?: { command: string; status: 'ok'|'skipped'|'failed'; reason?: string };  // Stage 2 の 1 回目（目的ごとに分ける）の記録（5.3）。どのコマンドで出したか
  llmText?: { command: string; status: 'ok'|'skipped'|'failed'; reason?: string };
  llmSpecs?: { command: string; status: 'ok'|'skipped'|'failed'; reason?: string };  // 要求の抜き出し（10.8）の記録。欄が無い = 走らせていない（チケットが無い・取れなかった、1 回目が ok でない、古い解析）  // 2 回目（文を書く）の記録（5.3）。欄が無い = 走らせていない（--no-llm、1 回目が ok でない、古い解析）
  llmIntents?: { command: string; status: 'ok'|'skipped'|'failed'; reason?: string };  // per-hunk intent (10.9). Absent if the first call was not ok, or in old analyses
  lsp?: { command: string; status: 'ok'|'unavailable'; reason?: string; waitedMs?: number };  // LSP の記録（9.1 / 9.5）。欄が無い = 動かしていない（--no-lsp か古い解析）
  structure?: {                              // 構成の推定の記録（10.6。2026-10-09）。欄が無い = 設定の layers を使った解析か古い解析
    status: 'ok'|'skipped'|'failed'; reason?: string; command?: string;
    inferred?: { headSha: string; at: string };   // ok のとき: 推定した時点
    layers?: string[];                            // ok のとき: 帯の並び（上が呼ぶ側）
    unclassified?: { count: number; total: number; warn: boolean; paths: string[] };  // 推定の後に増えた、どの層にも対象外にも入らないファイル
    tests?: string[];                             // テストの見分けに推定を使ったときだけ（5.2 の 7。2026-10-12）: 推定したテストの置き場
  };
  files?: FileText[];                        // head 側の全文。差分のあるファイル（削除を除く）と Symbol.path のファイル、path 順（2026-09-30）。古い解析には無い
  githubDiff?: Array<{ path: string; hunks: Array<{ oldStart: number; oldLines: number; newStart: number; newLines: number }> }>;  // GitHub の PR の差分の塊（12章。2026-10-06）。古い解析には無い
  mapGroups?: MapGroups;                     // groups on the home map (2026-11-04). Not used by the screen since 2026-11-05 (replaced by the flow map; removal comes later, ch. 17)
  behaviors?: Behavior[];                    // behaviors (2026-11-04). Not used by the screen since 2026-11-05 (same as above)
  flows?: CallFlow[];                        // request flows (2026-11-05). Call trees combining before and after the change. Absent in old analyses
}

// 入口の地図のまとまりの木（2026-11-04。packages/core/src/mapgroup/build.ts）。分け方はプログラム、題の名前だけ LLM（原則 3）。
// 箱はコースで分ける（箱の hunk の How の id のうち一番小さいもの = 地図の丸と同じ「最初のコース」）。1 枚に並べるのは 7 つまで（MAP_LIMIT）。
// - コースが 7 つ以下: 全景 = コース（How の並び）+ コース外
// - コースが 8 つ以上: 全景 = 1 回目の LLM の題（themes。10.2）+ コース外。題の中は、箱が 8 つ以上ならコース、7 つ以下なら箱。
//   題が規則（5.4 の 10）に合わなければ使わず、全景にコースを全部並べる
// - 箱の並びはコースの読む順（tours）に出てくる順、読む順に無い箱は path 順
// - 1 つのコースの箱が 8 つ以上のときの中の段は未実装（TODO。いまはそのまま並べる）
interface MapGroups { root: string[]; groups: MapGroup[]; boxes: MapBox[] }   // root = 全景に並べるまとまりの id
interface MapGroup {
  id: string;                                // 'theme:<n>' / 'course:<How の id>' / 'outside'
  kind: 'theme' | 'course' | 'outside';
  howId?: number;                            // course だけ
  title?: string;                            // theme だけ（LLM の名前。画面は点線）
  provenance: Provenance;                    // theme は llm、course と outside は ast
  children: string[];                        // 中に並ぶ MapGroup.id か MapBox.id（並び順）
}
interface MapBox {                           // 差分のあるファイル 1 つ（テストを除く）か、畳んだ束 1 つ
  id: string;                                // 'file:<path>' / 'group:<代表の hunk id>'
  label: string;                             // ファイルは拡張子を除いたファイル名、束は書き換えのパターン
  path?: string; hunkIds: string[];          // 束の hunkIds はメンバー全部。逸脱は自分のファイルの箱
  course: number;                            // 箱の最初のコース。0 = コース外
}

// Behaviors (2026-11-04. packages/core/src/behavior/): the diff's calls, from a start in calling order. Calling order, call expressions and return types come from the program; only the title comes from the LLM (principle 3).
// Decided by comparing mockups: the arrow text is the call expression (the screen folds arguments into 「…6」), returns are return types (facts, so solid lines), the start rule does not rely on layer names, and value cards are not shown (TODO in chapter 17).
// - Start = a symbol with a diff and no caller with a diff. A symbol with no outgoing call edge is not a behavior. Ordered by the start's layer order → FQ name
// - Follow call edges only, depth first. Within one caller, in order of the calling lines (Edge.lines); an edge with several lines gives one call per line. Enter each symbol once
// - Two calls with the same caller, line and name become one (interface and implementation; keep the one with a diff). Non-representative ripple members are not used
// - The call expression comes from reading the calling line with the syntax tree and picking a call whose name is on that line (the one named like the callee's last name, otherwise the outer one).
//   For non-Kotlin files, or when no expression is found, use the line's text (exact: false)
// The fixture has one behavior from OrderController.apply with 7 calls (execute → find → applyDiscount → rateFor → save → save → from)
interface Behavior {
  id: string;                                // 'b1' onward. Program order
  title: string; titleProvenance: Provenance; // llm = title from the second LLM call (dashed on screen). ast = no LLM, the start's short name
  start: string;                             // Symbol.id of the start. The screen first draws an arrow from the caller to here
  messages: Array<{
    from: string; to: string;                // Symbol.id
    line?: number;                           // calling line (head of the caller's file)
    call?: { text: string; exact: boolean; name?: string; args?: string[] };  // call expression (packed into one line). A trailing lambda is "{…}"
    returns?: string;                        // return type (Edge.types.out)
  }>;
}

// Request flows (2026-11-05. packages/core/src/flow/). Material for the map's ① Background / ② This PR / ③ After merge. As with behaviors, the program builds the tree and the LLM only writes the title and the per-stage sentences (principle 3).
// From the edges before the change (edgesBase) and after it (edges), build each caller's ordered calls per side (same as behaviors: one per line, interface and implementation merged into one) and match them.
// - Calls are matched as "the n-th call from the same caller to the same callee". Present on both sides: changed when a calling line falls on a changed line
```
